### Identity (reviewed)

Yves Lempereur’s preserved original Z80 assembly implementation for TRS-80 Model I/III.
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/README.md
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/ALI.ASM

### Classification (reviewed)

ALI.ASM implements alien movement, two-player/score state and phase handling; ALI1.ASM supplies instructions, sprite/state tables and 1982 MICRO 2000 author credits. Pinned LICENSE.md is PolyForm Internal Use License 1.0.0 with distribution restrictions; public visibility does not make this permissively licensed.
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/README.md
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/ALI.ASM

### Source CPU (reviewed)

Inspected implementation uses Z80 instructions, establishing source architecture independently of platform branding.
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/README.md
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/ALI.ASM

### Target CPU (reviewed)

Inspected native implementation/build establishes Z80 instruction-set target. This is not a verified minimum hardware configuration.
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/README.md
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/ALI.ASM

### Build (reviewed)

No modern build recipe or assembler invocation was found in the root inventory. Main assembly selects disk mode and contains tape/protected variants; includes and assembler macro dialect need reconstruction before a build can be claimed. Disk-selected source references SYSMACRO, absent from the pinned root tree; companion game ASM files are present. No source code was compiled or executed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/README.md
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/ALI.ASM

### Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found)

Native platform and processor instruction set are evidenced by source. Full hardware/OS minimum and runtime have not been independently verified.
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/README.md
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/ALI.ASM

### AI (no-evidence-found)

Unknown. No explicit development-AI attribution established in the inspected primary files; no inference of non-use.
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/README.md
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/ALI.ASM

### Relationships (reviewed)

Original-author TRS-80 game source release, corroborated by author page https://yves.lempereur.us/trs80.html. Separate title-specific implementation, not the author’s Macintosh emulator.
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/README.md
- https://github.com/ylempereur/AliensEggs/blob/6e6e2e19f886844a0059c3b1f7bf4b28878f100d/ALI.ASM

- https://github.com/DarwinNE/C64maze/blob/26366c16c8c0f88ae87fb5af9b10fd037f6caec8/Makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

c64maze.c implements map traversal, perspective rendering and timed exit objective. C64/C128 port files implement hardware bitmap/VDC access and SID music, including explicit 6502 inline assembly. Unix rendering is a separate SDL-backed port. README declares GPL v3; project LICENSE is present in root inventory.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/C64maze/blob/26366c16c8c0f88ae87fb5af9b10fd037f6caec8/README.md
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/C64maze/blob/26366c16c8c0f88ae87fb5af9b10fd037f6caec8/Makefile

### Source CPU (reviewed)

Inspected implementation uses 6502 instructions, establishing source architecture independently of platform branding.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/C64maze/blob/26366c16c8c0f88ae87fb5af9b10fd037f6caec8/README.md
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/C64maze/blob/26366c16c8c0f88ae87fb5af9b10fd037f6caec8/Makefile

### Target CPU (reviewed)

C64/C128 inline LDA/LDX/BIT/STX assembly and cc65 targets establish the 6502 family for those native outputs. Unix port CPU architecture is unknown; no host CPU support is inferred from C.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/C64maze/blob/26366c16c8c0f88ae87fb5af9b10fd037f6caec8/README.md
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/C64maze/blob/26366c16c8c0f88ae87fb5af9b10fd037f6caec8/Makefile

### Build (reviewed)

Makefile has cc65/ca65/ld65 targets for c64/c128 libraries and gcc plus SDL2/SDL2_ttf target for Unix. Checked-in PRG and D64 artifacts were inventoried, not executed. No source code was compiled or executed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/C64maze/blob/26366c16c8c0f88ae87fb5af9b10fd037f6caec8/README.md
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/C64maze/blob/26366c16c8c0f88ae87fb5af9b10fd037f6caec8/Makefile

### Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found)

Author describes native C64, C128 high-resolution VDC and Unix/SDL targets. Exact native RAM and host CPU minima were not verified.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/C64maze/blob/26366c16c8c0f88ae87fb5af9b10fd037f6caec8/README.md

Unknown. No explicit development-AI attribution established in the inspected primary files; no inference of non-use.

alien-inv.s has about 2,198 lines of original game implementation including alien bombs, cannon shots, movement, rendering and PAL-synchronised IRQ logic. ### Identity (reviewed)

Adventure Writing System-to-C translator, runtime support and retro-target examples by Davide Bucci.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/aws2c/blob/d8a9a08b7cb9164a35ac4783f4c2692d82e944b0/README.md
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/aws2c/blob/d8a9a08b7cb9164a35ac4783f4c2692d82e944b0/makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

aws2c.c is a substantive roughly 4,798-line parser/code generator with vocabulary, conditions, text compression and generated configuration handling. README explicitly warns AWS 3.2 actions/functions are not all supported and translation errors can yield uncompilable output. cc65 retro examples are part of this coherent compiler project. No whole-project licence conclusion established from inspected files; historical AWS example games have separate provenance.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/aws2c/blob/d8a9a08b7cb9164a35ac4783f4c2692d82e944b0/README.md
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/aws2c/blob/d8a9a08b7cb9164a35ac4783f4c2692d82e944b0/makefile

Input is Adventure Writing System text data rather than machine code; no source CPU applies.

makefile compiles compress.o and aws2c.o with host C compiler. README documents input .aws to generated .c and downstream C/runtime compilation. ### Identity (reviewed)

Preservation and Glass-assembler adaptation of ANMA’s original 1992 Frantic Z80 game source, with recovery of lost sections and annotated audio replay.
- https://github.com/stompetower/frantic/blob/2198fe7fd8568aa167616e1e55787cbd9f1a4b3d/README.md
- https://github.com/stompetower/frantic/blob/2198fe7fd8568aa167616e1e55787cbd9f1a4b3d/make

### Classification (reviewed)

src/game.asm contains player motion/jump and world logic, and the extensively commented replayer implements PSG/FM effects. Companion assets, six stage definitions and C# map extractor remain part of one coherent archive. MODIFICATIONS.md records PSG safety correction, removed bad-sector protection and changed trailing padding, so a blanket byte-identical claim would be wrong. README declares Mozilla Public License 2.0 and root LICENSE is present.
- https://github.com/stompetower/frantic/blob/2198fe7fd8568aa167616e1e55787cbd9f1a4b3d/README.md
- https://github.com/stompetower/frantic/blob/2198fe7fd8568aa167616e1e55787cbd9f1a4b3d/make

- https://github.com/stompetower/frantic/blob/2198fe7fd8568aa167616e1e55787cbd9f1a4b3d/make

### Build (reviewed)

The make script invokes bundled Glass 0.5 Java assembler for loader, intro, game, ending, replayer and six stage files, combines map/stage data and copies music/graphics to dsk. README attributes builds to Java runtime and documents MSX BLOAD boot. No source code was compiled or executed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.
- https://github.com/stompetower/frantic/blob/2198fe7fd8568aa167616e1e55787cbd9f1a4b3d/README.md
- https://github.com/stompetower/frantic/blob/2198fe7fd8568aa167616e1e55787cbd9f1a4b3d/make

### Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found)

README specifies MSX2 or higher, at least 64 KB RAM memory mapper, 128 KB VRAM and a 720 KB floppy drive for real hardware; independent execution and configuration validation not performed.

src/ECBBAS.SA implements BASIC storage/stack/interpreter routines with original Microsoft 1982 copyright. Repository preserves scanned/OCR/listing/source stages plus non-original common definitions for lwtools. README describes missing conditional/macro material and credits correction; this is original-listing recovery, not chiefly a ROM disassembly. LICENSE states historical archival/reference purposes; Microsoft hosting permission and Eurohard R&D director release are documented, not a general permissive licence.

Use consumes full memory-card image and wipes card contents; deliberately corrupt filesystem can crash console/card managers. No card writing/flashing or code execution attempted. Author model/BIOS support statements not independently verified.

Wonderful WSwan — native WonderSwan system libraries. ## Research event notes

Broad discovery followed platform catalogues, developer portfolios, source-preservation and tooling links, with programmatic URL screening before detailed inspection. This is a diverse bounded pass, not an exhaustive inventory of every platform. Quality and completeness caveats remain explicit; preserved source, native reconstruction, homebrew and modern tooling are distinguished. No candidate code was executed. All earlier catalogue records are preserved.
